    <div style="visibility:hidden;" data-view-component="true" class="UnderlineNav-actions js-responsive-underlinenav-overflow position-absolute pr-3 tmp-pr-3 pr-md-4 tmp-pr-md-4 pr-lg-5 tmp-pr-lg-5 right-0">      <action-menu data-select-variant="none" data-view-component="true">

    <div id="repo-content-pjax-container" class="repository-content " >
    



    
      
  <div class="clearfix container-xl tmp-px-5 tmp-mt-4 page" data-hpc="true" id="wiki-wrapper">
    <div class="d-flex flex-column flex-md-row gh-header">
      <h1 class="flex-auto min-width-0 mb-2 mb-md-0 mr-0 mr-md-2 gh-header-title">AddressSanitizerAlgorithm</h1>

        <div class="mt-0 mt-lg-1 flex-shrink-0 gh-header-actions">
          <a href="#wiki-pages-box" class="d-md-none ">Jump to bottom</a>

        </div>
    </div>

      <div class="mt-2 mt-md-1 tmp-pb-3 gh-header-meta">
        Mitch Phillips edited this page <relative-time datetime="2017-10-04T00:22:29Z" class="no-wrap">Oct 4, 2017</relative-time>
        &middot;
        <a href="/google/sanitizers/wiki/AddressSanitizerAlgorithm/_history" data-view-component="true" class="Link Link--underline">5 revisions</a>
      </div>

    <div id="wiki-content" class="tmp-mt-4">
      <div data-view-component="true" class="Layout Layout--flowRow-until-md Layout--sidebarPosition-end Layout--sidebarPosition-flowRow-end">
  <div data-view-component="true" class="Layout-main">          <div id="wiki-body" class="gollum-markdown-content">
              <div class="markdown-body">
                <div class="markdown-heading"><h1 class="heading-element">Short version</h1><a id="user-content-short-version" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Short version" href="#short-version"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>The run-time library replaces the <code>malloc</code> and <code>free</code> functions.
The memory around malloc-ed regions (red zones) is poisoned.
The <code>free</code>-ed memory is placed in quarantine and also poisoned.
Every memory access in the program is transformed by the compiler in the following way:</p>
<p>Before:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="*address = ...;  // or: ... = *address;"><pre class="notranslate"><code>*address = ...;  // or: ... = *address;
</code></pre></div>
<p>After:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="if (IsPoisoned(address)) {
  ReportError(address, kAccessSize, kIsWrite);
}
*address = ...;  // or: ... = *address;"><pre class="notranslate"><code>if (IsPoisoned(address)) {
  ReportError(address, kAccessSize, kIsWrite);
}
*address = ...;  // or: ... = *address;
</code></pre></div>
<p>The tricky part is how to implement <code>IsPoisoned</code> very fast and <code>ReportError</code> very compact.
Also, instrumenting some of the accesses may be <a href="AddressSanitizerCompileTimeOptimizations">proven redundant</a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h1 class="heading-element">Memory mapping and Instrumentation</h1><a id="user-content-memory-mapping-and-instrumentation" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Memory mapping and Instrumentation" href="#memory-mapping-and-instrumentation"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>The virtual address space is divided into 2 disjoint classes:</p>
<ul>
<li>Main application memory (<code>Mem</code>): this memory is used by the regular application code.</li>
<li>Shadow memory (<code>Shadow</code>): this memory contains the shadow values (or metadata).
There is a correspondence between the shadow and the main application memory.
<strong>Poisoning</strong> a byte in the main memory means writing some special value into the corresponding shadow memory.</li>
</ul>
<p>These 2 classes of memory should be organized in such a way that computing the shadow memory
(<code>MemToShadow</code>) is fast.</p>
<p>The instrumentation performed by the compiler:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="shadow_address = MemToShadow(address);
if (ShadowIsPoisoned(shadow_address)) {
  ReportError(address, kAccessSize, kIsWrite);
}"><pre class="notranslate"><code>shadow_address = MemToShadow(address);
if (ShadowIsPoisoned(shadow_address)) {
  ReportError(address, kAccessSize, kIsWrite);
}
</code></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">Mapping</h2><a id="user-content-mapping" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Mapping" href="#mapping"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p><a href="AddressSanitizer">AddressSanitizer</a> maps 8 bytes of the application
memory into 1 byte of the shadow memory.</p>
<p>There are only 9 different values for any aligned 8 bytes of the application memory:</p>
<ul>
<li>All 8 bytes in qword are unpoisoned (i.e. addressable). The shadow value is 0.</li>
<li>All 8 bytes in qword are poisoned (i.e. not addressable). The shadow value is negative.</li>
<li>First <code>k</code> bytes are unpoisoned, the rest <code>8-k</code> are poisoned. The shadow value is <code>k</code>.
This is guaranteed by the fact that <code>malloc</code> returns 8-byte aligned chunks of memory.
The only case where different bytes of an aligned qword have different state is the tail of
a malloc-ed region. For example, if we call <code>malloc(13)</code>, we will have one full unpoisoned
qword and one qword where 5 first bytes are unpoisoned.</li>
</ul>
<p>The instrumentation looks like this:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="byte *shadow_address = MemToShadow(address);
byte shadow_value = *shadow_address;
if (shadow_value) {
  if (SlowPathCheck(shadow_value, address, kAccessSize)) {
    ReportError(address, kAccessSize, kIsWrite);
  }
}"><pre class="notranslate"><code>byte *shadow_address = MemToShadow(address);
byte shadow_value = *shadow_address;
if (shadow_value) {
  if (SlowPathCheck(shadow_value, address, kAccessSize)) {
    ReportError(address, kAccessSize, kIsWrite);
  }
}
</code></pre></div>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="// Check the cases where we access first k bytes of the qword
// and these k bytes are unpoisoned.
bool SlowPathCheck(shadow_value, address, kAccessSize) {
  last_accessed_byte = (address &amp; 7) + kAccessSize - 1;
  return (last_accessed_byte &gt;= shadow_value);
}"><pre class="notranslate"><code>// Check the cases where we access first k bytes of the qword
// and these k bytes are unpoisoned.
bool SlowPathCheck(shadow_value, address, kAccessSize) {
  last_accessed_byte = (address &amp; 7) + kAccessSize - 1;
  return (last_accessed_byte &gt;= shadow_value);
}
</code></pre></div>
<p><code>MemToShadow(ShadowAddr)</code> falls into the <code>ShadowGap</code> region
which is unaddressable. So, if the program tries to directly access a memory location
in the shadow region, it will crash.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 class="heading-element">64-bit</h3><a id="user-content-64-bit" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 64-bit" href="#64-bit"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="Shadow = (Mem &gt;&gt; 3) + 0x7fff8000;"><pre class="notranslate"><code>Shadow = (Mem &gt;&gt; 3) + 0x7fff8000;
</code></pre></div>

<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 class="heading-element">32 bit</h3><a id="user-content-32-bit" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 32 bit" href="#32-bit"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">Ultra compact shadow</h2><a id="user-content-ultra-compact-shadow" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Ultra compact shadow" href="#ultra-compact-shadow"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>It is possible to use even more compact shadow memory, e.g.</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="Shadow = (Mem &gt;&gt; 7) | kOffset;"><pre class="notranslate"><code>Shadow = (Mem &gt;&gt; 7) | kOffset;
</code></pre></div>
<p>Experiments are in flight.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">Report Error</h2><a id="user-content-report-error" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Report Error" href="#report-error"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>The <code>ReportError</code> could be implemented as a call (this is the default now),
but there are some other, slightly more efficient and/or more compact solutions.
At some point the default behaviour <strong>was</strong>:</p>
<ul>
<li>copy the failure address to <code>%rax</code> (<code>%eax</code>).</li>
<li>execute <code>ud2</code> (generates SIGILL)</li>
<li>Encode access type and size in a one-byte instruction which follows <code>ud2</code>.
Overall these 3 instructions require 5-6 bytes of machine code.</li>
</ul>
<p>It is possible to use just a single instruction (e.g. <code>ud2</code>), but this will require
to have a full disassembler in the run-time library (or some other hacks).</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">Stack</h2><a id="user-content-stack" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Stack" href="#stack"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>In order to catch stack buffer overflow, <a href="AddressSanitizer">AddressSanitizer</a> instruments the code like this:</p>
<p>Original code:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="void foo() {
  char a[8];
  ...
  return;
}"><pre class="notranslate"><code>void foo() {
  char a[8];
  ...
  return;
}
</code></pre></div>
<p>Instrumented code:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="void foo() {
  char redzone1[32];  // 32-byte aligned
  char a[8];          // 32-byte aligned
  char redzone2[24];
  char redzone3[32];  // 32-byte aligned
  int  *shadow_base = MemToShadow(redzone1);
  shadow_base[0] = 0xffffffff;  // poison redzone1
  shadow_base[1] = 0xffffff00;  // poison redzone2, unpoison 'a'
  shadow_base[2] = 0xffffffff;  // poison redzone3
  ...
  shadow_base[0] = shadow_base[1] = shadow_base[2] = 0; // unpoison all
  return;
}"><pre class="notranslate"><code>void foo() {
  char redzone1[32];  // 32-byte aligned
  char a[8];          // 32-byte aligned
  char redzone2[24];
  char redzone3[32];  // 32-byte aligned
  int  *shadow_base = MemToShadow(redzone1);
  shadow_base[0] = 0xffffffff;  // poison redzone1
  shadow_base[1] = 0xffffff00;  // poison redzone2, unpoison 'a'
  shadow_base[2] = 0xffffffff;  // poison redzone3
  ...
  shadow_base[0] = shadow_base[1] = shadow_base[2] = 0; // unpoison all
  return;
}
</code></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">Examples of instrumented code (x86_64)</h2><a id="user-content-examples-of-instrumented-code-x86_64" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Examples of instrumented code (x86_64)" href="#examples-of-instrumented-code-x86_64"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">Unaligned accesses</h2><a id="user-content-unaligned-accesses" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Unaligned accesses" href="#unaligned-accesses"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>The current compact mapping will not catch unaligned partially out-of-bound accesses:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="int *x = new int[2]; // 8 bytes: [0,7].
int *u = (int*)((char*)x + 6);
*u = 1;  // Access to range [6-9]"><pre class="notranslate"><code>int *x = new int[2]; // 8 bytes: [0,7].
int *u = (int*)((char*)x + 6);
*u = 1;  // Access to range [6-9]
</code></pre></div>
<p>A viable solution is described in <a href="https://github.com/google/sanitizers/issues/100">https://github.com/google/sanitizers/issues/100</a> but it comes at a performance cost.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h1 class="heading-element">Run-time library</h1><a id="user-content-run-time-library" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Run-time library" href="#run-time-library"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">Malloc</h2><a id="user-content-malloc" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Malloc" href="#malloc"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>The run-time library replaces <code>malloc</code>/<code>free</code> and provides error reporting functions like <code>__asan_report_load8</code>.</p>
<p><code>malloc</code> allocates the requested amount of memory with redzones around it.
The shadow values corresponding to the redzones are poisoned
and the shadow values for the main memory region are cleared.</p>
<p><code>free</code> poisons shadow values for the entire region and puts the chunk of memory
into a quarantine queue (such that this chunk
will not be returned again by malloc during some period of time).</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h1 class="heading-element">Comments?</h1><a id="user-content-comments" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Comments?" href="#comments"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
